Could use "exact 2-coordinate descent with top-peak pair moves" as a polishing method. Let's design efficient and simple.

Data structures:
- a: np.float64 length n, sum=1.
- conv: fast_convolve(a) length 2n-1.
- M = conv.max().
- best score = 2n*M (since sum=1) or use evaluate for verification.

Move operation: move δ from source j to target i (a_i +=δ, a_j -=δ). The affected indices are union of [i, i+n-1] and [j, j+n-1] clipped to [0,2n-2]. But we can update conv after accepted move incrementally.

To evaluate a candidate (i,j,δ), we can compute new_conv only for affected indices using formula new_c = c + Lδ+Qδ^2, where L_k=2(a_{k-i} - a_{k-j}) (with out-of-range 0), Q as above. Need include for all k in affected union. We can vectorize:
For move target i, source j, δ.
Define arrays for affected range maybe full length 2n-1; but we can compute new full conv? That would be O(2n) per candidate; if n=1024, 2k. 100k candidates =>200M, maybe okay in numpy if vectorized? But Python loop over candidates with array ops length 2n might be slow but acceptable for few thousand.

Better: precompute for each coordinate i the affected range k_i_start=i, k_i_end=i+n-1. For pair (i,j), union start min(i,j), end max(i,j)+n-1. Length up to 2n. We can create slice l,r. For candidate δ, need a_{k-i} and a_{k-j} for k in slice. This is a shifted view of a, may wrap? For k in slice, k-i can be outside 0..n-1. We can compute using np.zeros? For performance, can precompute full-length arrays shifted_i of length 2n-1: sh_i = [0]*(n-1) + a + [0]*(n-1)? Let's find: For a coordinate i, values B_k = a_{k-i} if 0≤k-i≤n-1 else0. For k=0..2n-2. This is a shifted copy: B = zeros(2n-1); B[i:i+n]=a. So B_i. Similarly B_j. Then L=2(B_i - B_j) over affected slice. Q: zeros full length; Q[2i]=1, Q[2j]=1, if i+j<2n-1 Q[i+j]+=-2 (if i≠j); if i+j equals 2i? no. If i+j equals 2j? no. If 2i or 2j equal i+j with i=j only. If 2i might also equal i+j? j=i no. If i+j equals 2j? i=j no. If 2i==2j no. If i+j same as 2i? no. So Q can be assigned full length arrays per candidate? Creating zeros length2n per candidate too heavy. Instead compute affected slice and add Q values if positions in slice: new_c = c_slice + L_slice*δ; then adjust specific positions: qpos=2i,2j,i+j; if in slice, new_c[pos-start] += Q*δ^2. Need L_slice. B_i/B_j full arrays memory: n=1024, 2n-1=2047; B for all n => 1024*2047 ~2M floats=16MB, okay. For n=4096, 16M=128MB maybe high. We can limit n to 1024 or 2048. Parent length 1024. Choose n=1024 for 2D moves. Could also use n=2048 with no B matrix and compute slices via take, but okay maybe. B matrix lets candidate eval fast.

But move evaluation for many candidates: For each pair (i,j), L = 2*(B[i][l:r] - B[j][l:r]). δ array maybe multiple. We can compute max for a set of δ vectorized: new_vals = c_slice[None,:] + L[None,:]*δ[:,None] + Q adjustments not easy vector if few Q positions. We can handle Q by adding to candidate max after. For a pair, Q has up to 3 positions; can initialize max_new = max(unaff_max, c affected + Lδ) and then for Q positions adjust specific values for each δ. But easier: create small 2D array shape len(δ)*len(slice), if len δ=8, len slice~2000 =>16k floats, okay per candidate. If 10k candidates =>160M operations in C, maybe okay few seconds? Python overhead may dominate.

We can generate candidate pairs and δ lists, evaluate batch? Could be complex.

Simplify: For each top peak, choose a few source-target pairs and evaluate a few δ via scalar loop. Use vector per pair. Need enough moves.

Candidate generation:
- Active peaks: indices where conv > M - threshold, maybe top K=20.
- For each peak k, find contributing coordinates p where a_p a_{k-p} large. Source should be one of p or k-p with large a. Target should be coordinate with small a and where moving reduces L at this and other active peaks. We can precompute for each coordinate i its gradient g_i = Σ_active w_k ∂c_k/∂a_i = 2Σ w_k a_{k-i}. Moving mass from high g to low g can reduce weighted peaks. Actually if we move δ from source j to target i, linear change at peak k = 2δ(a_{k-i}-a_{k-j}). For active set, weighted change =2δ(g_i - g_j) where g_i =Σ w_k a_{k-i}. To reduce weighted peaks, choose i with low g, j with high g. This is like gradient descent direction. We can compute g for current weights (softmax over active peaks). Then candidate pairs (i low g, j high g). Then update direction maybe. This is efficient: compute g length n in O(K*n) or FFT. Then select top 10 high g sources and bottom 10 low g targets. For each pair (i in low, j in high), try δ fractions of a_j. This will reduce active peaks. But may increase other peaks; evaluation catches.

This combination of gradient direction + exact pairwise move is promising. It's essentially Frank-Wolfe / negative gradient with coordinate pair moves preserving sum. It can escape single-coordinate issues.

Algorithm "pair_gradient_descent":
1. Normalize a sum1, compute conv, M.
2. Choose active peak weights: top K peaks (e.g. 50) with softmax temperature scaled to M. Compute g_i =2 Σ w_k a_{k-i}. Could compute by loop over peaks: for each k, valid i range, add w_k*2*a[k-i]. For n=1024,K=50, negligible.

Let's detail pair_descent implementation:

```
def build_shifts(a):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    B=np.zeros((n,N), dtype=np.float64)
    rows=np.arange(n)
    for i in range(n):
        B[i, i:i+n] = a
    return B
```
Loop O(n^2) for n=2048 ~4M assignments, okay. Could vectorize: B[rows, rows[:,None]+np.arange(n)] = a[None,:]? That creates n*n array. Simpler loop? Python loop 2048, each slice assignment, okay maybe 0.01s. For n=4096, 16M, okay.

```
def compute_gradient(a, conv, K=32, p=4):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    if K>=N: peaks=np.arange(N); w=np.ones(N)/N
    else:
        peaks=np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
        pv=conv[peaks]
        # softmax or power weights
        # use power to focus top; normalize
        w=(pv / max(pv.max(),1e-300))**p
        ws=w.sum(); if ws<...: w=...
        w=w/ws
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx,k in enumerate(peaks):
       wv=w[idx]
       if wv<1e-15: continue
       i_start=max(0,k-n+1); i_end=min(n-1,k)
       # g[i_start:i_end+1] += 2*wv * a[k - i_end : k - i_start + 1][::-1]
       seg = a[k - i_end : k - i_start + 1]
       g[i_start:i_end+1] += 2.0*wv*seg[::-1]
    return g
```
Check derivative factor 2 included.

Move evaluation:
```
def evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    l=min(i,j); r=min(max(i,j)+n, N)
    c_slice=conv[l:r]
    if B is not None:
       L=2.0*(B[i,l:r] - B[j,l:r])
    else:
       L=2.0*(get_shift_slice(a,i,l,r) - get_shift_slice(a,j,l,r))
    # unaff
    if l>0: ma=conv[:l].max() else ma=-1.0
    if r<N: mb=conv[r:].max() else mb=-1.0
    unaff=max(ma,mb)
    best_delta=0; best_M=M?
    # To avoid allocating 2D for each? We can loop deltas small (8) and use 1D array.
    for d in deltas:
       if d<=0 or d>a[j]+1e-15: continue
       new = c_slice + L*d
       if 2*i>=l and 2*i<r: new[2*i-l] += d*d  # But new is view? c_slice + L*d creates copy.
       if 2*j>=l and 2*j<r: new[2*j-l] += d*d
       if i+j>=l and i+j<r and i!=j: new[i+j-l] -= 2*d*d
       m=float(new.max())
       if m<unaff: m=unaff
       if m<best_M - 1e-15: best_M=m; best_delta=d
    return best_delta,best_M
```
But note if Q positions overlap (e.g., 2*i == i+j? only if i=j; no; 2*i ==2*j if i=j), okay. If two Q positions same? no. If i+j maybe equals 2i or2j no. If i+j >N-1? Since i,j<n, i+j≤2n-2 valid.

Potential issue: c_slice + L*d creates new array length slice; doing for each delta allocates. We can precompute L and for deltas use maybe for d. Deltas count small. Fine.

After accepting best move, update conv:
```
def apply_move(a,B,i,j,d):
   if d==0: return
   n=len(a); N=2*n-1
   l=min(i,j); r=min(max(i,j)+n,N)
   # L=...
   if B is not None: L=2*(B[i,l:r]-B[j,l:r])
   else: ...
   conv[l:r] += L*d
   d2=d*d
   pos=2*i; if pos<N: conv[pos] += d2
   pos=2*j; if pos<N: conv[pos] += d2
   if i!=j:
      pos=i+j
      if pos<N: conv[pos] -= 2*d2
   # careful: if pos=2i and also pos=i+j? no; if i=0 j? fine.
   a[i]+=d; a[j]=max(a[j]-d,0.0)
   # update B diagonals if B not none
```
But wait: When updating conv with L*d, L was computed from old B (old a). We then adjust a. If we adjust conv before B, okay. But if q positions overlap with L update? The formula includes linear + quadratic. For pos 2i, L already includes 2(a_i - a_{2i-j})d; adding d^2 correct. For pos i+j, L includes 2(a_j-a_i)d; adding -2d^2 correct. Good.

Update B after a? B formula uses old? If we update B after a changes by delta, for each coord c: B[p,p+c] = a_c. So add delta to diagonal for i, subtract for j. Order doesn't matter. Use row_indices.
```
def update_shifts(B, coord, delta):
   n=B.shape[0]
   rows=np.arange(n) # maybe global precompute
   cols=rows+coord
   B[rows, cols] += delta
```
Need rows+coord < N? coord≤n-1, rows max n-1, sum 2n-2=N-1, okay.

But if B is None, use get_shift_slice:
```
def get_shift_slice(a, coord, l, r):
   # return array length r-l of a[k-coord] for k in [l,r)
   n=len(a)
   out=np.zeros(r-l)
   start=l-coord; end=r-coord
   s=max(0,start); e=min(n,end)
   if s<e:
       out[s-start:e-start] = a[s:e]
   return out
```
This allocates; for B None maybe slower. We can use B for n<=2048.

Need initialize shifts after a changes from interpolation etc.

def get_source_target_indices(n,g,a,K_src=12,K_tgt=12,rng=None,extra_random=0):
    active = a > 1e-15
    # source candidates: all indices sorted by g desc, take active. If too few, include top a.
    src_order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
    src=[]
    for idx in src_order:
       if active[idx] and idx not in src: src.append(idx)
       if len(src)>=K_src: break
    # target candidates: sorted by g asc
    tgt_order=np.argsort(g)
    tgt=[]
    for idx in tgt_order:
       if idx not in tgt: tgt.append(idx)
       if len(tgt)>=K_tgt: break
    # ensure sources distinct from targets; if all sources also in targets, okay skip same.
    # add random: sample low random targets and high random sources
```

Top imports: numpy, time, maybe scipy.fft? We can use np.fft for speed no scipy dependency? Use numpy only to be safe. `np.fft.rfft` available. For n up to 2048, use np.fft. Custom fast_convolve:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fft_len=1<<(size-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fft_len)[:size]
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0) # remove tiny negative? Convolution nonnegative; clip.
    return out
```
Clipping tiny negative may slightly alter? Okay. Could not clip to preserve accuracy? Max unaffected. But small negative outside? Clip.

`evaluate_sequence` may be available; use final few times. But calling it many times with np.convolve O(n^2) maybe okay for <100. Internal score uses fast.

Normalize:
```
def normalize_prob(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-15: a=np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64); s=1
    else: a/=s
    return a
```

`internal_score(a)`:
```
s=a.sum(); if s<... return inf
conv=fast_convolve(a); return 2*len(a)*conv.max()/(s*s)
```

`build_shifts` loop:
```
def build_shifts(a):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    B=np.zeros((n,N), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        B[i, i:i+n] = a
    return B
```
Can vectorize with `idx = np.arange(n); B[idx, idx[:,None]+np.arange(n)] = a[None,:]` but that creates n*n boolean/index arrays. For n=2048, 4M indices memory ~64MB, okay but loop simpler. Loop 2048 slice assignments C, fast.

`update_shifts`:
```
def update_shifts(B, coord, delta):
    n=B.shape[0]
    rows = np.arange(n) # Maybe create global? But top-level cannot closures; can compute each call. To avoid repeated arange, pass rows? Use global constant? We can define `ROWS_CACHE`? No global mutable maybe. Simpler compute.
    cols = rows + coord
    B[rows, cols] += delta
```
Creating arange each apply (could be many) overhead. We can precompute `row_idx = np.arange(n)` in pair_descent and pass to update? Helper signature `update_shifts(B, coord, delta, rows)`. Good.
```
def update_shifts(B, coord, delta, rows):
    B[rows, rows+coord] += delta
```
`rows+coord` creates array length n each update. unavoidable but okay. Could precompute cols for each coord? Too memory. Fine.

If no B, get_shift_slice:

def get_shift_slice(a, coord, l, r):
    n=len(a); out=np.zeros(r-l)
    start=l-coord; end=r-coord
    if start<0:
       e=min(n,end); if e>0: out[:e] = a[:e]? Need align.
    ...
```
Let's implement robust:
We need for k∈[l,r), idx=k-coord. Want output[t]=a[idx] if 0≤idx<n.
`start = l - coord`, `end = r - coord` (half-open indices in a to map).
`t_start=0`. The slice of output where idx ranges 0..n-1: t from start to end. Intersection with [0,len). 
```
s_out = max(0, start)
e_out = min(r-l, end)
if s_out < e_out:
    a_idx_start = s_out - start
    a_idx_end = e_out - start
    out[s_out:e_out] = a[a_idx_start:a_idx_end]
```
Yes because output index t corresponds idx=t+start. If t in [s_out,e_out), idx in [s_out-start, e_out-start), valid.

`outside_max`:
```
def outside_max(conv, l, r, N):
    ma = -1.0
    if l > 0:
       val = conv[:l].max()
       if val > ma: ma=val
    if r < N:
       val = conv[r:].max()
       if val > ma: ma=val
    return ma
```

`evaluate_move` top-level:
Signature `evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas, N, rows?)`. B maybe None. It doesn't need rows.
```
def evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas, N):
    if i == j: return None
    aj = a[j]
    if aj <= 1e-15: return (0.0, inf?)
    # filter deltas: >0, <=aj
    ok = (deltas > 1e-15) & (deltas <= aj + 1e-15)
    if not np.any(ok): return (0.0, float('inf'))
    dts = np.minimum(deltas[ok], aj)
    l = min(i,j); r = max(i,j)+n; if r>N: r=N
    c_slice = conv[l:r]
    if B is not None:
        L = 2.0 * (B[i, l:r] - B[j, l:r])
    else:
        L1 = get_shift_slice(a, i, l, r)
        L2 = get_shift_slice(a, j, l, r)
        L = 2.0 * (L1 - L2)
    vals = c_slice[None, :] + L[None, :] * dts[:, None]
    # q adjustments
    q1=2*i; if q1<N: vals[:, q1-l] += dts*dts (if q1 in slice)
    q2=2*j ...
    if i != j: q3=i+j; vals[:, q3-l] -= 2*dts*dts if q3 in slice
    m_vals = vals.max(axis=1)
    unaff = outside_max(conv,l,r,N)
    if unaff > 0? Actually unaff can be -1 if no outside; but M positive. Use if unaff > m_vals: m_vals = np.maximum(m_vals, unaff)
    # if unaff -1, maximum with -1 no issue.
    bad = m_vals >= current_best? We don't know. Return min.
    idx = int(np.argmin(m_vals))
    return dts[idx], float(m_vals[idx])
```
Need `n` from len(a), N=2n-1 provided.
Potential bug: If q1==q2 (i=j) skipped. If q3-l index maybe same as q1? not for i≠j. If q1-l out of slice, condition.
`dts*dts` compute once.
If `unaff` is -1.0 and all vals maybe? But conv nonnegative, M positive. If l=0,r=N, unaff=-1; maximum with -1 okay. Use `if unaff > -0.5: m_vals = np.maximum(m_vals, unaff)`.

But if `vals` max includes unaffected inside slice? All inside could change; unaffected outside only. Good.

Local refinement: after best pair/d, call `evaluate_move` with `deltas` list of fine values around d. But it will again search. We can create list and pass.

`apply_move`:
```
def apply_move(a, B, conv, i,j,d, rows):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    if d==0: return
    l=min(i,j); r=max(i,j)+n; if r>N: r=N
    if B is not None:
       L = 2.0*(B[i,l:r] - B[j,l:r])
    else: ...
    conv[l:r] += L*d
    d2=d*d
    q=2*i; if q<N: conv[q] += d2
    q=2*j; if q<N: conv[q] += d2
    if i != j:
       q=i+j; if q<N: conv[q] -= 2*d2
    a[i] += d
    newj = a[j] - d
    if newj < 0: newj=0.0
    a[j]=newj
    if B is not None:
       update_shifts(B, i, d, rows)
       update_shifts(B, j, -d, rows)
```
If after clipping a[j] not zero? d≤a[j], no.
Important: We update conv using old B; then update B. But we also update a before B? Order: compute L from old B; update conv; then update a; then update B. Good.

If B updated after a, but if newj clipped slightly negative due floating, B subtract d while actual a[j] maybe not exactly -d? If d≤a[j]+1e-15, could make negative tiny; we set to0; B subtract d => inconsistency if a[j]-d <0. Better choose d = min(d, a[j]) exactly before. In evaluate, `dts = np.minimum(deltas[ok], aj)`. If aj positive, okay. Use same d. If due floating a[j] changes, a[i] update, a[j] = max(a[j]-d,0). The delta for j is actual old - new = maybe slightly less than d if clipping. But d≤aj so no clipping except roundoff. To be safe, before apply: `if d > a[j]: d = a[j]`. Then actual delta_j = a[j] - (a[j]-d)=d. Good.

`pair_descent` detailed:
```
def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=True, label=""):
    a = normalize_prob(a0)
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    rows=np.arange(n)
    conv=fast_convolve(a); M=float(conv.max())
    B=build_shifts(a) if n <= 2048 else None
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0; accepts=0; no_improve=0; last_rebuild=t0
    # hyperparams adapt
    K_src=8; K_tgt=8; K_peak=32; power=4
    coarse_fracs = (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8)
    # if time large, maybe more
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if M < best_M - 1e-14:
          best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
       # maybe if best_M internal <= internal target? no
       # compute gradient
       g=compute_gradient(a, conv, K_peak, power)
       # select sources/targets
       # Use argpartition
       if K_src < n:
           src_part=np.argpartition(g, n-K_src)[-K_src:]
       else: src_part=np.arange(n)
       src_order=np.argsort(g[src_part])[::-1]
       src_list=[]
       for idx in src_order:
           idx=int(idx)
           if a[idx] > 1e-14 and idx not in src_list: src_list.append(idx)
           if len(src_list)>=2*K_src: break
       # top a extension
       if len(src_list) < K_src:
           a_order=np.argsort(a)[::-1]
           for idx in a_order:
              idx=int(idx)
              if a[idx]>1e-14 and idx not in src_list: src_list.append(idx)
              if len(src_list)>=K_src: break
       if K_tgt < n:
           tgt_part=np.argpartition(g, K_tgt)[:K_tgt]
       else: tgt_part=np.arange(n)
       tgt_order=np.argsort(g[tgt_part])
       tgt_list=[]
       for idx in tgt_order:
          idx=int(idx)
          if idx not in tgt_list: tgt_list.append(idx)
          if len(tgt_list)>=2*K_tgt: break
       # low a extension? maybe
       if len(tgt_list) < K_tgt:
           a_order2=np.argsort(a)
           for idx in a_order2:
              idx=int(idx)
              if idx not in tgt_list: tgt_list.append(idx)
              if len(tgt_list)>=K_tgt: break
       # trim to K_src/K_tgt maybe 8 each
       src_list=src_list[:K_src]
       tgt_list=tgt_list[:K_tgt]
       if not src_list or not tgt_list:
           # random sources/targets
           ...
       best_d=0.0; best_i=-1; best_j=-1; best_newM=M
       # candidate loop
       for i in tgt_list:
          for j in src_list:
             if i==j: continue
             if a[j] <= 1e-15: continue
             deltas = a[j] * coarse_fracs
             d,newM = evaluate_move(a,B,conv,i,j,deltas,N)
             if newM < best_newM - 1e-15:
                 best_newM=newM; best_d=d; best_i=i; best_j=j
       # refine best pair if found
       if best_i >=0 and best_d>0:
           # fine deltas around best_d and a_j fractions
           aj=a[best_j]
           fine=[]
           for f in (best_d*0.5, best_d*0.75, best_d*0.9, best_d*1.1, best_d*1.3, best_d*1.6, aj*0.01, aj*0.03, aj*0.1, aj*0.3, aj*0.6, aj*0.95):
              if f>1e-16 and f<=aj: fine.append(f)
           if fine:
              d2,m2=evaluate_move(a,B,conv,best_i,best_j,np.array(fine),N)
              if m2 < best_newM - 1e-15:
                  best_newM=m2; best_d=d2
       if best_i >=0 and best_newM < M - 1e-15:
           apply_move(a,B,conv,best_i,best_j,best_d,rows)
           M=float(conv.max())
           accepts+=1
           no_improve=0
           it+=1
           # if accept makes M worse due numeric, update best? no
           if best_newM < best_M - 1e-14:
               best_M=best_newM; best_a=a.copy()
           # periodic rebuild
           if accepts % 300 == 0 or time.monotonic()-last_rebuild > 5.0:
               conv2=fast_convolve(a); B2=None
               if B is not None: B2=build_shifts(a)
               conv=conv2; B=B2; M=float(conv.max()); last_rebuild=time.monotonic()
               if M < best_M: best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
           no_improve+=1; it+=1
           # Handle stagnation
           if no_improve > 100:
               # random kick: accept if not too bad
               kick_scale=0.002
               # try random moves
               improved_or_kick=False
               for _ in range(50):
                   j=int(rng.integers(n)); i=int(rng.integers(n))
                   if i==j or a[j]<=1e-15: continue
                   deltas = a[j]*np.array((0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5))
                   d,rM=evaluate_move(a,B,conv,i,j,deltas,N)
                   if rM < M - 1e-15:
                      apply...
                      improved_or_kick=True; break
                   if rM < M*(1+kick_scale):
                      # accept kick
                      apply...
                      improved_or_kick=True
                      break
               if not improved_or_kick:
                   # noise reset
                   noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * (0.01/np.sqrt(n))
                   a2=np.maximum(a+noise,0)
                   s=a2.sum()
                   if s>1e-12: a2/=s
                   conv2=fast_convolve(a2); M2=float(conv2.max())
                   if M2 < best_M or M2 < M*(1+0.01):
                      a=a2; conv=conv2; M=M2
                      if B is not None: B=build_shifts(a)
               no_improve=0
               # maybe adjust hyperparameters
               K_peak = int(min(64, max(16, K_peak*1.2)))
               power = max(2, power-1) if power>2 else 4
               # alternate source/target counts? 
```
Need be careful: In stagnation, if we accept a kick that increases M, best_M remains. no_improve reset. But current M may be higher; subsequent strict descent from current. Good.

Function `make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n, alpha, bump_type)`:
```
x = np.arange(1,n+1, dtype=float)
base = 1.0/np.sqrt(x)
base /= base.sum()
if alpha >=1:
   a=base
else:
   t = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if bump_type=='right_taper':
      # support t>0.5, taper to zero at 1: (t-0.5)*(1-t)
      b = np.where(t>0.5, (t-0.5)*(1-t), 0.0)
   elif 'flat': b=np.ones(n)
   elif 'gauss': b=np.exp(-0.5*((t-0.75)/0.15)**2)
   b=np.maximum(b,0); bsum=b.sum(); if bsum>0: b/=bsum
   a = alpha*base + (1-alpha)*b
   a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()
return a
```

`make_init` functions:
```
def make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n, alpha, bump_type):
    x = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    base = 1.0 / np.sqrt(x)
    bs=base.sum()
    if bs>0: base/=bs
    if alpha >= 1.0:
        return base
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if bump_type == 'right_taper':
        b = np.zeros(n)
        mask = t > 0.45
        tt = t[mask]
        b[mask] = (tt - 0.45) * (1.0 - tt)
    elif bump_type == 'right_gauss':
        b = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - 0.8) / 0.12)**2)
    elif bump_type == 'flat':
        b = np.ones(n)
    else:
        b = np.zeros(n); b[-1]=1
    b=np.maximum(b,0); bsum=b.sum()
    if bsum <= 0:
        b=np.ones(n); bsum=n
    b/=bsum
    a=alpha*base + (1-alpha)*b
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<... a=base; else a/=s
    return a
```
Maybe alpha should be less than1 to add mass. If alpha=0.98, base mass 0.98, bump 0.02.

```
def interpolate_sequence(seq, n_new):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64); m=seq.size
    if m==n_new: return normalize_prob(seq)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    v=np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq)
    v=np.maximum(v,0)
    return normalize_prob(v)
```

   a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)

Detailed pair_descent code with no syntax errors:

```
def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="", K_peak=48, power=4, src_count=8, tgt_count=8):
    a = normalize_prob(a0)
    n = len(a)
    N = 2*n - 1
    rows = np.arange(n)
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    if n <= 2500:
        B = build_shifts(a)
    else:
        B = None
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    accepts = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_rebuild = t0
    if n > 1500:
       coarse_fracs = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.70)
       if src_count > 6: src_count = 6

    fine_multipliers = (0.5,0.75,0.9,1.1,1.3,1.6)
    fine_fracs = (0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5,0.95)
    max_iter = 0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
       max_iter += 1
       if M < best_M - 1e-14:
          best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
       # compute gradient
       g = compute_gradient(a, conv, K_peak, power)
       # select sources
       if n <= src_count:
           src_candidates = np.arange(n)
       else:
           src_part = np.argpartition(g, n - src_count * 2)[-src_count*2:]
       src_order = np.argsort(g[src_candidates])[::-1] if n>src_count else src_candidates
```

`select_source_target`:
```
def select_source_target(a, g, src_count, tgt_count):
    n = len(a)
    if src_count > n: src_count=n
    if tgt_count > n: tgt_count=n
    if src_count == 0 or tgt_count == 0: return [],[]
    if src_count < n:
       src_part = np.argpartition(g, n - src_count)[-src_count:]
    else:
       src_part = np.arange(n)
    src_order = np.argsort(g[src_part])[::-1]
    src_list=[]
    for idx in src_order:
       idx=int(idx)
       if a[idx] > 1e-14 and idx not in src_list:
          src_list.append(idx)
       if len(src_list) >= src_count: break
    if len(src_list) < src_count:
       # use global top a
       a_order = np.argsort(a)[::-1]
       for idx in a_order:
          idx=int(idx)
          if a[idx] > 1e-14 and idx not in src_list:
             src_list.append(idx)
          if len(src_list) >= src_count: break
    if tgt_count < n:
       tgt_part = np.argpartition(g, tgt_count)[:tgt_count]
    else:
       tgt_part=np.arange(n)
    tgt_order=np.argsort(g[tgt_part])
    tgt_list=[]
    for idx in tgt_order:
       idx=int(idx)
       if idx not in tgt_list:
          tgt_list.append(idx)
       if len(tgt_list) >= tgt_count: break
    if len(tgt_list) < tgt_count:
       a_order=np.argsort(a)
       for idx in a_order:
          idx=int(idx)
          if idx not in tgt_list:
             tgt_list.append(idx)
          if len(tgt_list) >= tgt_count: break
    return src_list, tgt_list
```
This uses `idx in src_list` O(count) fine.

Could include top `2*count` source candidates to ensure active. But if many zeros, full top a. Good.

`compute_gradient` top-level:
```
def compute_gradient(a, conv, K, power):
    n=len(a); N=len(conv)
    if K <=0: K=1
    if K >= N:
        peaks=np.arange(N)
        w=np.ones(N)/N
    else:
        peaks=np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
        pv=conv[peaks]
        mx=pv.max()
        if mx <=0:
           w=np.ones(K)/K
        else:
           w=(pv/mx)**power
           ws=w.sum()
           if ws<=0: w=np.ones(K)/K
           else: w=w/ws
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for ii in range(len(peaks)):
       wv=w[ii]
       if wv <= 1e-16: continue
       k=int(peaks[ii])
       i_start=max(0, k-n+1)
       i_end=min(n-1, k)
       count=i_end-i_start+1
       if count <=0: continue
       # segment a[k-i_end : k-i_start+1]
       seg = a[k - i_end : k - i_start + 1]
       g[i_start:i_end+1] += (2.0*wv) * seg[::-1]
    return g
```
If K much smaller. Good.

`evaluate_move` uses `n` from len(a); if `deltas` list has values >aj, we min. But if all <=0, returns None? Let's return `(0.0, float('inf'))`.

```
def evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas, N):
    n=len(a)
    aj=a[j]
    if aj <= 1e-15 or i == j:
        return 0.0, float('inf')
    deltas = np.asarray(deltas, dtype=np.float64)
    ok = (deltas > 1e-16) & (deltas <= aj + 1e-15)
    if not np.any(ok): return 0.0, float('inf')
    dts = deltas[ok]
    dts[dts > aj] = aj
    l = i if i<j else j
    r = (i if i>j else j) + n
    if r > N: r = N
    if r <= l: return 0.0, float('inf')
    c_slice = conv[l:r]
    if B is not None:
       L = 2.0 * (B[i,l:r] - B[j,l:r])
    else:
       L = 2.0 * (get_shift_slice(a,i,l,r) - get_shift_slice(a,j,l,r))
    vals = c_slice[None, :] + L[None, :] * dts[:, None]
    d2 = dts * dts
    q = 2*i
    if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
       vals[:, q-l] += d2
    q = 2*j
    if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
       vals[:, q-l] += d2
    if i != j:
       q = i+j
       if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
          vals[:, q-l] -= 2.0*d2
    m_vals = vals.max(axis=1)
    unaff = outside_max(conv, l, r, N)
    if unaff > -0.5:
       # use where unaff > m_vals
       m_vals = np.maximum(m_vals, unaff)
    idx=int(np.argmin(m_vals))
    return float(dts[idx]), float(m_vals[idx])
```
Note if `unaff` > -0.5 but m_vals maybe all lower; `np.maximum` with scalar works. If unaff=-1, no. If conv values can be 0? M positive; unaff -1 only no outside. If outside exists but max 0 (impossible if sum1 but maybe some conv zero), unaff=0 > -0.5, max with 0. Good.

Potential issue: `values` includes changed inside slice but if outside max is unaff, okay. But what if q position outside? no.

`apply_move` should use same r. If r=N, etc.
```
def apply_move(a, B, conv, i, j, d, rows):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1
    if d <= 0: return
    if d > a[j]: d=a[j]
    l = min(i,j); r = max(i,j) + n; if r>N: r=N
    if r <= l: return
    if B is not None:
       L = 2.0*(B[i,l:r] - B[j,l:r])
    else: L=...
    conv[l:r] += L*d
    d2=d*d
    q=2*i
    if q<N: conv[q] += d2
    q=2*j
    if q<N: conv[q] += d2
    if i != j:
       q=i+j
       if q<N: conv[q] -= 2*d2
    a[i] += d
    a[j] = a[j] - d
    if a[j] < 0: a[j] = 0.0
    if B is not None:
       update_shifts(B, i, d, rows)
       update_shifts(B, j, -d, rows)
```
If a[j] clipped, B update still -d may over-subtract if d was set to a[j] before; if a[j]-d =0 exactly no over. Due float, maybe -1e-18; we set to0; B subtract d leads B value -1e-18? Since B stored a[j] old; old - d maybe -eps; okay small. Could set d = min(d, a[j]*(1-1e-15)) to avoid negative. Not needed.

But if a[i] target was same as source? skip.

`build_shifts` after many updates may have tiny negative on diagonals due clipping? Rebuild periodically corrects.

Now, consider if pair_descent uses `best_M` from incremental M but current `a` sum maybe not exactly 1 due floating after many moves. Moves preserve sum within roundoff; noise normalize. For internal score, sum drift negligible. We can every rebuild normalize? Rebuild after fast_convolve; if sum drift, maybe normalize a to sum1 before rebuild to keep score. But pair moves sum preserve; normalization would change conv and B and could improve? We can in rebuild: `a_sum=a.sum(); if abs(a_sum-1)>1e-12: a/=a_sum`. This might slightly change objective but okay. Do before conv. In apply, sum remains. In noise, normalized. So okay.

else: a = a/s
conv=fast_convolve(a)

```

def propose_candidate():
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 1780.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240525)
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float('inf')
    # parent
    parent = None
    try:
       p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if p.ndim !=1: p=p.ravel()
       p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
       p = np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
       if p.size>0:
          p = p / p.sum()
          parent = p
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    # consider parent
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       seq = normalize_prob(parent)
       try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
       except: sc=float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
          print(...)
    if best_seq is None:
       seq = make_inverse_sqrt_mix(1024, 1.0, 'flat')
       sc=...
       best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
    # Main optimization from best_seq (parent)
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60:
       main_budget = min(remaining * 0.62, 1000.0)
       a_opt = pair_descent(best_seq, main_budget, rng, verbose=True, label="main", K_peak=64, power=4, src_count=6, tgt_count=6)
       seq = normalize_prob(a_opt)
       sc = ...
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_seq=seq
       print final
    # Alternative if time left
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    n_best = len(best_seq)
    # Maybe try a tuned inverse sqrt mix if score not very good or time >250
    if remaining > 250 and np.isfinite(best_score):
       # Generate a few, pick best internal score
       cands = []
       cands.append(make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_best, 0.98, 'right_taper'))
       cands.append(make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_best, 0.97, 'right_gauss'))
       cands.append(make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_best, 0.95, 'right_taper'))
       # maybe if best_score > 1.5027, include pure invsqrt
       cands.append(make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_best, 1.0, 'flat'))
       # evaluate internal scores quickly
       best_alt=None; best_alt_int=inf; best_alt_true=inf
       for c in cands:
          try: sc_int=internal_score(c)
          except: sc_int=inf
          if sc_int < best_alt_int - 1e-9:
             best_alt_int=sc_int; best_alt=c
       if best_alt is not None and (best_alt_int < best_score * 1.02 + 1e-12 or best_alt_int < 1.55):
          budget = min(remaining * 0.35, 350.0)
          if budget > 60:
             a_opt = pair_descent(best_alt, budget, rng, verbose=True, label="alt_inv", K_peak=64, power=3, src_count=6, tgt_count=6)
             seq=normalize_prob(a_opt); sc=...
             if sc < best_score: update
    # Try interpolation to 2n if time left
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 350 and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 2000:
       n_new = len(best_seq)*2
       if n_new <= 3072:
          alt = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, n_new)
          # internal score
          sc_int=internal_score(alt)
          if sc_int < best_score * 1.05 + 1e-9:
             budget = min(remaining * 0.5, 600.0)
             if budget > 120:
                a_opt = pair_descent(alt, budget, rng, verbose=True, label=f"interp{n_new}", K_peak=64, power=4, src_count=5, tgt_count=5)
                seq=normalize_prob(a_opt); sc=...
                if sc < best_score: update
    # Final polish
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 80 and best_seq is not None:
       budget = min(remaining * 0.7, 300.0)
       if budget > 30:
          a_opt = pair_descent(best_seq, budget, rng, verbose=True, label="final", K_peak=96, power=2, src_count=9, tgt_count=9)
          seq=normalize_prob(a_opt); sc=...
          if sc < best_score: update
    # final verify
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq = make_inverse_sqrt_mix(1024,1.0,'flat')
    else:
       final = normalize_prob(best_seq)
       try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(final.tolist()))
       except: sc=best_score
       if np.isfinite(sc): best_score=sc; best_seq=final
    print("FINAL SCORE:", best_score, "len", len(best_seq))
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return inf
    conv=fast_convolve(a)
    return float(2.0*len(a)*conv.max()/(s*s))
```
For normalized, sum1.

Potential issue: `make_inverse_sqrt_mix(1024,1.0,'flat')` with alpha=1 returns base, internal score maybe 1.57. Good.

Should we set array length for inverse mix equal to best_seq length, which may be 2048 if we eventually updated best to 2048. If alternative before interp uses n_best=1024. Fine.

It is local; after function returns, reference count zero, CPython frees memory to allocator, may keep in process but reuse. Memory peak okay.

```
def normalize_prob(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
       return np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)/max(len(a),1)
    return a/s
```

def try_multi_move(a, conv, src_list, tgt_list, alpha):
    n=len(a); N=len(conv)
    h=min(3, len(src_list)); l=min(3, len(tgt_list))
    if h==0 or l==0: return None,None,float('inf')
    H=src_list[:h]
    L=tgt_list[:l]
    amts=np.array([a[j] for j in H])
    d=alpha * float(np.min(amts))
    if d <= 1e-16: return None,None,float('inf')
    delta=np.zeros(n)
    for j in H: delta[j] -= d
    add = h*d / float(l)
    for i in L: delta[i] += add
    a2=a+delta
    # if target same as source? H and L may overlap if small n; if overlap, delta may cancel. Need ensure distinct? In pair, skip same; multi if overlap, moving from and to same net partly. Could filter L not in H_set. Build H_set set(H); if len(L_with filter)==0 return. For small n, okay.
    # If some H had a_j < d? d min*alpha <= min, alpha<=1, ok.
    a2=np.maximum(a2,0)
    s=a2.sum()
    if s <= 0: return None
    conv2=fast_convolve(a2)
    M2=float(conv2.max())
    return a2, conv2, M2
```

Let's add helper `simplex_project` (top-level) and in stagnation try a few lr.

`simplex_project` standard.
```
def simplex_project(v):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return np.ones(1)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond=u*rho > (cssv-1.0)
    if np.any(cond):
       k=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
       theta=(cssv[k]-1.0)/(k+1.0)
       return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    return np.ones(n)/n
```
In pair_descent, after no_improve > 50 perhaps, try:
```
gmax = g.max()
if gmax > 0:
  for lr in (0.001,0.005,0.02,0.08)/gmax? Actually g magnitude ~values ~1/n? Need scale.
```
Since a sum1, g = 2Σw a_{...}, each term ≤? sum over active maybe 1, g_i ≤~2. So gmax O(1). Euclidean projection step lr around 0.01? We can try `lr = alpha / max(gmax,1e-12)` with alpha in [0.01,0.05,0.2,0.8]. This is like normalized. `v=a - lr*g`. If gmax=0 skip. Evaluate fast_convolve. Accept if improves or small kick. This is a full multicoord move and may improve more. Add helper `try_gradient_step(a,g,conv,alpha)`. But no need top-level? We'll add maybe in stagnation directly using helpers.

       # multi move
       ...
    if not accepted and no_improve > 200:
       # reset best + noise
    no_improve=0
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function that minimizes
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2
using exact 2-coordinate "mass transfer" descent, periodic restarts,
full simplex-projected gradient kicks, and occasional longer-sequence
interpolation/polishing.  The method maintains the sum exactly at 1 and
uses FFT plus precomputed shifted copies for fast exact move evaluation.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def fast_convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if size <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    fft_len = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fft_len)[:size]
    # FFT roundoff can produce tiny negatives.
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def normalize_prob(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(a.size, 1)
    a /= s
    return a


def internal_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float('inf')
    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * float(conv.max()) / (s * s))


def build_shifts(a):
    n = len(a)
    N = 2 * n - 1
    B = np.zeros((n, N), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        B[i, i:i + n] = a
    return B


def update_shifts(B, coord, delta, rows):
    # B[t, t + coord] = a[coord]
    B[rows, rows + coord] += delta


def get_shift_slice(a, coord, l, r):
    n = len(a)
    length = r - l
    out = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
    start = l - coord
    end = r - coord
    s_out = max(0, start)
    e_out = min(length, end)
    if s_out < e_out:
        a_s = s_out - start
        a_e = e_out - start
        if 0 <= a_s < a_e <= n:
            out[s_out:e_out] = a[a_s:a_e]
    return out


def outside_max(conv, l, r, N):
    best = -1.0
    if l > 0:
        v = float(conv[:l].max())
        if v > best:
            best = v
    if r < N:
        v = float(conv[r:].max())
        if v > best:
            best = v
    return best


def compute_gradient(a, conv, K, power):
    n = len(a)
    N = len(conv)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= N:
        peaks = np.arange(N, dtype=int)
        w = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(N)
    else:
        peaks = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
        peaks = np.asarray(peaks, dtype=int)
        pv = conv[peaks]
        mx = float(pv.max())
        if mx <= 0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
            w = np.ones(len(peaks), dtype=np.float64)
            w /= float(len(peaks))
        else:
            w = (pv / mx) ** max(1, int(power))
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.ones(len(peaks), dtype=np.float64)
                w /= float(len(peaks))
            else:
                w /= ws

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for ii in range(len(peaks)):
        wv = float(w[ii])
        if wv <= 1e-16:
            continue
        k = int(peaks[ii])
        i_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i_end = min(n - 1, k)
        cnt = i_end - i_start + 1
        if cnt <= 0:
            continue
        seg = a[k - i_end:k - i_start + 1]
        g[i_start:i_end + 1] += (2.0 * wv) * seg[::-1]
    return g


def select_source_target(a, g, src_count, tgt_count):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return [], []
    if src_count <= 0 or tgt_count <= 0:
        return [], []
    src_count = min(src_count, n)
    tgt_count = min(tgt_count, n)

    if src_count < n:
        src_part = np.argpartition(g, n - src_count)[-src_count:]
    else:
        src_part = np.arange(n)
    src_order = np.argsort(g[src_part])[::-1]
    src_list = []
    for idx in src_order:
        idx = int(idx)
        if a[idx] > 1e-14 and idx not in src_list:
            src_list.append(idx)
        if len(src_list) >= src_count:
            break

    if len(src_list) < src_count:
        a_order = np.argsort(a)[::-1]
        for idx in a_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if a[idx] > 1e-14 and idx not in src_list:
                src_list.append(idx)
            if len(src_list) >= src_count:
                break

    if tgt_count < n:
        tgt_part = np.argpartition(g, tgt_count)[:tgt_count]
    else:
        tgt_part = np.arange(n)
    tgt_order = np.argsort(g[tgt_part])
    tgt_list = []
    for idx in tgt_order:
        idx = int(idx)
        if idx not in tgt_list:
            tgt_list.append(idx)
        if len(tgt_list) >= tgt_count:
            break

    if len(tgt_list) < tgt_count:
        a_order = np.argsort(a)
        for idx in a_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in tgt_list:
                tgt_list.append(idx)
            if len(tgt_list) >= tgt_count:
                break

    if not src_list:
        src_list = [int(np.argmax(a))]
    if not tgt_list:
        tgt_list = [0]

    return src_list, tgt_list


def evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas, N):
    n = len(a)
    if i == j:
        return 0.0, float('inf')
    aj = float(a[j])
    if aj <= 1e-15:
        return 0.0, float('inf')

    deltas = np.asarray(deltas, dtype=np.float64)
    ok = (deltas > 1e-16) & (deltas <= aj + 1e-15)
    if not np.any(ok):
        return 0.0, float('inf')

    dts = deltas[ok].copy()
    dts[dts > aj] = aj

    l = min(i, j)
    r = max(i, j) + n
    if r > N:
        r = N
    if r <= l:
        return 0.0, float('inf')

    c_slice = conv[l:r]
    if B is not None:
        L = 2.0 * (B[i, l:r] - B[j, l:r])
    else:
        L = 2.0 * (get_shift_slice(a, i, l, r) - get_shift_slice(a, j, l, r))

    vals = c_slice[None, :] + L[None, :] * dts[:, None]
    d2 = dts * dts

    q = 2 * i
    if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
        vals[:, q - l] += d2

    q = 2 * j
    if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
        vals[:, q - l] += d2

    if i != j:
        q = i + j
        if 0 <= q < N and l <= q < r:
            vals[:, q - l] -= 2.0 * d2

    m_vals = vals.max(axis=1)
    unaff = outside_max(conv, l, r, N)
    if unaff > -0.5:
        m_vals = np.maximum(m_vals, unaff)

    idx = int(np.argmin(m_vals))
    return float(dts[idx]), float(m_vals[idx])


def apply_move(a, B, conv, i, j, d, rows):
    n = len(a)
    N = 2 * n - 1
    d = float(d)
    if d <= 0 or i == j:
        return
    if d > a[j]:
        d = float(a[j])
    if d <= 0:
        return

    l = min(i, j)
    r = max(i, j) + n
    if r > N:
        r = N
    if r <= l:
        return

    if B is not None:
        L = 2.0 * (B[i, l:r] - B[j, l:r])
    else:
        L = 2.0 * (get_shift_slice(a, i, l, r) - get_shift_slice(a, j, l, r))

    conv[l:r] += L * d
    d2 = d * d

    q = 2 * i
    if q < N:
        conv[q] += d2

    q = 2 * j
    if q < N:
        conv[q] += d2

    if i != j:
        q = i + j
        if q < N:
            conv[q] -= 2.0 * d2

    a[i] += d
    a[j] = a[j] - d
    if a[j] < 0.0:
        a[j] = 0.0

    if B is not None:
        update_shifts(B, i, d, rows)
        update_shifts(B, j, -d, rows)


def simplex_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0)
    if np.any(cond):
        k = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (cssv[k] - 1.0) / (k + 1.0)
        out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
        s = out.sum()
        if s > 0:
            out /= s
        else:
            out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return out
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n


def try_gradient_step(a, g, conv, alpha):
    gmax = float(g.max())
    if gmax <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gmax):
        return None, None, float('inf')
    v = a - (float(alpha) / gmax) * g
    a2 = simplex_project(v)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None, None, float('inf')
    a2 /= s
    conv2 = fast_convolve(a2)
    M2 = float(conv2.max())
    return a2, conv2, M2


def try_multi_move(a, src_list, tgt_list, alpha):
    n = len(a)
    if not src_list or not tgt_list:
        return None, None, float('inf')

    h = min(3, len(src_list))
    l = min(3, len(tgt_list))
    if h <= 0 or l <= 0:
        return None, None, float('inf')

    H = src_list[:h]
    L = tgt_list[:l]
    amounts = np.array([float(a[j]) for j in H], dtype=np.float64)
    d = float(alpha) * float(np.min(amounts))
    if d <= 1e-16 or not np.isfinite(d):
        return None, None, float('inf')

    delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in H:
        delta[j] -= d
    add = float(h) * d / float(l)
    for i in L:
        delta[i] += add

    a2 = a + delta
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None, None, float('inf')
    a2 /= s
    conv2 = fast_convolve(a2)
    M2 = float(conv2.max())
    return a2, conv2, M2


def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="",
                 K_peak=64, power=4, src_count=6, tgt_count=6):
    a = normalize_prob(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1:
        return a
    N = 2 * n - 1
    rows = np.arange(n, dtype=int)

    conv = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(conv.max())
    if n <= 2300:
        B = build_shifts(a)
    else:
        B = None

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    accepts = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_rebuild = t0
    last_src = []
    last_tgt = []

    if n > 1500:
        src_count = min(src_count, 6)
        tgt_count = min(tgt_count, 6)
        coarse_arr = np.array((0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.70), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        coarse_arr = np.array((0.03, 0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.60, 0.85), dtype=np.float64)

    fine_multipliers = (0.55, 0.75, 0.90, 1.10, 1.30, 1.60)
    fine_fracs = (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.90)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit - 1.0:
            break

        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()

        g = compute_gradient(a, conv, K_peak, power)
        src_list, tgt_list = select_source_target(a, g, src_count, tgt_count)
        last_src = src_list
        last_tgt = tgt_list

        best_newM = M
        best_i = -1
        best_j = -1
        best_d = 0.0

        for i in tgt_list:
            for j in src_list:
                if i == j:
                    continue
                aj = float(a[j])
                if aj <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                deltas = aj * coarse_arr
                d, newM = evaluate_move(a, B, conv, i, j, deltas, N)
                if d > 0 and newM < best_newM - 1e-15:
                    best_newM = newM
                    best_i = i
                    best_j = j
                    best_d = d

        if best_i >= 0 and best_d > 0:
            aj = float(a[best_j])
            fine_vals = []
            for mult in fine_multipliers:
                v = best_d * mult
                if v > 1e-16 and v <= aj:
                    fine_vals.append(v)
            for f in fine_fracs:
                v = aj * f
                if v > 1e-16 and v <= aj:
                    fine_vals.append(v)
            if fine_vals:
                fine_arr = np.array(fine_vals, dtype=np.float64)
                fine_arr = np.unique(fine_arr)
                d2, m2 = evaluate_move(a, B, conv, best_i, best_j, fine_arr, N)
                if d2 > 0 and m2 < best_newM - 1e-15:
                    best_newM = m2
                    best_d = d2

        if best_i >= 0 and best_newM < M - 1e-15:
            if best_d > a[best_j]:
                best_d = float(a[best_j])
            apply_move(a, B, conv, best_i, best_j, best_d, rows)
            M = float(conv.max())
            accepts += 1
            no_improve = 0

            if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()

            if accepts % 200 == 0 or time.monotonic() - last_rebuild > 8.0:
                a = normalize_prob(a)
                conv = fast_convolve(a)
                B = build_shifts(a) if B is not None else None
                M = float(conv.max())
                last_rebuild = time.monotonic()
                if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                    best_M = M
                    best_a = a.copy()

            if verbose and accepts % 100 == 0:
                elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
                print(f"    [{label}] accepts={accepts} noimp={no_improve} "
                      f"M={M:.12f} score={2.0 * n * best_M:.8f} ({elapsed:.0f}s)")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 300:
                # Reset to best and perturb.
                a = best_a.copy()
                conv = fast_convolve(a)
                B = build_shifts(a) if B is not None else None
                M = float(conv.max())
                for _ in range(20):
                    scale = 0.02 / np.sqrt(float(n))
                    a2 = a + rng.standard_normal(n) * scale
                    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
                    s2 = float(a2.sum())
                    if s2 <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    a2 /= s2
                    conv2 = fast_convolve(a2)
                    M2 = float(conv2.max())
                    if M2 < M * (1.0 + 0.01) or M2 < best_M:
                        a = a2
                        conv = conv2
                        B = build_shifts(a) if B is not None else None
                        M = M2
                        break
                no_improve = 0

                K_peak = int(min(128, max(16, int(K_peak * 1.3))))
                if power > 2:
                    power = 2
                else:
                    power = 4
                src_count = int(min(12, max(4, src_count + 1)))
                tgt_count = int(min(12, max(4, tgt_count + 1)))
                if n > 1500:
                    src_count = min(src_count, 6)
                    tgt_count = min(tgt_count, 6)

            elif no_improve % 50 == 0:
                accepted = False

                # Strict full-vector projected gradient kick.
                for alpha in (0.02, 0.08, 0.30):
                    a2, conv2, M2 = try_gradient_step(a, g, conv, alpha)
                    if a2 is not None and M2 < M - 1e-14:
                        a = a2
                        conv = conv2
                        B = build_shifts(a) if B is not None else None
                        M = M2
                        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        no_improve = 0
                        accepted = True
                        break

                if not accepted and no_improve % 150 == 0:
                    # Directed multi-coordinate mass transfer and tiny upward kick.
                    for alpha in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
                        a2, conv2, M2 = try_multi_move(a, last_src, last_tgt, alpha)
                        if a2 is not None and (M2 < M - 1e-14 or M2 < M * (1.0 + 0.004)):
                            a = a2
                            conv = conv2
                            B = build_shifts(a) if B is not None else None
                            M = M2
                            if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                                best_M = M
                                best_a = a.copy()
                            no_improve = 0
                            accepted = True
                            break

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(f"    [{label}] it={it} accepts={accepts} noimp={no_improve} "
                  f"best_score={2.0 * n * best_M:.8f} ({elapsed:.0f}s)")

    return best_a


def make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n, alpha, bump_type):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    base = 1.0 / np.sqrt(x)
    bs = float(base.sum())
    if bs <= 0:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        base /= bs

    alpha = float(alpha)
    if alpha >= 1.0:
        return normalize_prob(base)
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        alpha = 0.0

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if bump_type == 'right_taper':
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = t > 0.45
        tt = t[mask]
        b[mask] = (tt - 0.45) * (1.0 - tt)
    elif bump_type == 'right_gauss':
        b = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - 0.80) / 0.12) ** 2)
    elif bump_type == 'left_taper':
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = t < 0.55
        tt = t[mask]
        b[mask] = (0.55 - tt) * tt
    elif bump_type == 'flat':
        b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b[-1] = 1.0

    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bs = float(b.sum())
    if bs <= 0:
        b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bs = float(n)
    b /= bs

    a = alpha * base + (1.0 - alpha) * b
    return normalize_prob(a)


def make_cos_init(n, phi, amp):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(np.pi * x + float(phi))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return normalize_prob(a)


def interpolate_sequence(seq, n_new):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = seq.size
    if m <= 0:
        return normalize_prob(np.ones(1))
    n_new = max(1, int(n_new))
    if m == n_new:
        return normalize_prob(seq)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    v = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.nan_to_num(seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0))
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    return normalize_prob(v)


def transform_candidates(seq):
    seq = normalize_prob(seq)
    cands = [seq]

    cands.append(seq[::-1])
    cands.append(0.5 * (seq + seq[::-1]))

    if seq.size >= 5:
        k3 = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        k3 /= k3.sum()
        cands.append(np.convolve(seq, k3, mode='same'))

        k5 = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        k5 /= k5.sum()
        cands.append(np.convolve(seq, k5, mode='same'))

    # Mild shape power transforms, clipped by normalization.
    p_lo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(seq, 1e-12), 1.25))
    cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(seq, 1e-12), 0.80))

    out = []
    for c in cands:
        c = normalize_prob(c)
        if c.size == seq.size and np.isfinite(c).all():
            out.append(c)
    return out


def propose_candidate():
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 1780.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240525)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float('inf')

    # Load parent if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0:
            parent = normalize_prob(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Pick a good seed among parent and simple transforms / analytic seeds.
    seed_source = parent
    if seed_source is None:
        seed_source = make_inverse_sqrt_mix(1024, 1.0, 'flat')

    seed_source = normalize_prob(seed_source)
    seed_cands = transform_candidates(seed_source)

    # Also try a few inverse-sqrt mixture starts at the seed length.
    n_seed = len(seed_source)
    analytic_seeds = [
        make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_seed, 0.99, 'right_taper'),
        make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_seed, 0.97, 'right_taper'),
        make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_seed, 0.98, 'right_gauss'),
        make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_seed, 0.95, 'right_taper'),
        make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_seed, 1.0, 'flat'),
    ]

    all_start_cands = list(seed_cands) + analytic_seeds

    best_start = seed_source
    best_start_int = internal_score(seed_source)
    for cand in all_start_cands:
        cand = normalize_prob(cand)
        if len(cand) != n_seed:
            continue
        try:
            sc_int = internal_score(cand)
        except Exception:
            sc_int = float('inf')
        if np.isfinite(sc_int) and sc_int < best_start_int - 1e-12:
            best_start_int = sc_int
            best_start = cand

    # Record true scores for all cheap seeds.
    for cand in [seed_source, best_start] + analytic_seeds:
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = float('inf')
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = normalize_prob(cand)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = best_start
        best_score = internal_score(best_seq)

    print(f"Initial best: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Main local search from the best seed.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 80:
        budget = min(remaining * 0.60, 950.0)
        a_opt = pair_descent(best_seq, budget, rng, verbose=True, label="main",
                             K_peak=64, power=4, src_count=6, tgt_count=6)
        a_opt = normalize_prob(a_opt)
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = float('inf')
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a_opt
            print(f"After main: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Try a dedicated inverse-sqrt-mixture start if it looks competitive.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 240 and best_seq is not None:
        n_cur = len(best_seq)
        backs = [
            make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_cur, 0.98, 'right_taper'),
            make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_cur, 0.96, 'right_gauss'),
            make_inverse_sqrt_mix(n_cur, 0.94, 'right_taper'),
        ]
        best_back = None
        best_back_int = float('inf')
        for b in backs:
            try:
                bi = internal_score(b)
            except Exception:
                bi = float('inf')
            if np.isfinite(bi) and bi < best_back_int - 1e-12:
                best_back_int = bi
                best_back = b

        if best_back is not None and (best_back_int < best_score * 1.02 + 1e-9 or best_back_int < 1.54):
            budget = min((deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35, 320.0)
            if budget > 60:
                a_opt = pair_descent(best_back, budget, rng, verbose=True, label="alt_inv",
                                     K_peak=72, power=3, src_count=6, tgt_count=6)
                a_opt = normalize_prob(a_opt)
                try:
                    sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist()))
                except Exception:
                    sc = float('inf')
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = a_opt
                    print(f"After alt_inv: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Try doubling length from the current best (interpolation) if time permits.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 360 and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 2000:
        n_new = len(best_seq) * 2
        if n_new <= 3072:
            alt = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, n_new)
            try:
                alt_int = internal_score(alt)
            except Exception:
                alt_int = float('inf')
            if np.isfinite(alt_int) and alt_int < best_score * 1.05 + 1e-9:
                budget = min((deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.55, 620.0)
                if budget > 120:
                    a_opt = pair_descent(alt, budget, rng, verbose=True,
                                         label=f"interp{n_new}",
                                         K_peak=64, power=4, src_count=5, tgt_count=5)
                    a_opt = normalize_prob(a_opt)
                    try:
                        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist()))
                    except Exception:
                        sc = float('inf')
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_seq = a_opt
                        print(f"After interp{n_new}: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Final polish on global best.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 90 and best_seq is not None:
        budget = min(remaining * 0.72, 320.0)
        if budget > 30:
            a_opt = pair_descent(best_seq, budget, rng, verbose=True, label="final",
                                 K_peak=96, power=2, src_count=9, tgt_count=9)
            a_opt = normalize_prob(a_opt)
            try:
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                sc = float('inf')
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a_opt
                print(f"After final polish: score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(best_seq)}")

    # Final safe normalization and verification.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_inverse_sqrt_mix(1024, 1.0, 'flat')
    else:
        final_seq = normalize_prob(best_seq)
        try:
            final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            final_score = best_score
        if np.isfinite(final_score):
            best_score = final_score
            best_seq = final_seq
        else:
            best_seq = final_seq

    print(f"FINAL SCORE: {best_score:.8f} (n={len(best_seq)})")
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
